I mean, my concentration seems okay. When I'm at work, I can still focus on the systems and get my tasks done. It's just the tiredness that gets to me, but my actual ability to concentrate hasn't really changed that I've noticed.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself worrying excessively about several different areas of your life, not just work?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean, I worry about work sometimes, like I mentioned with the client tickets when I wake up early, but that's about it. I'm not sitting around worrying about a bunch of other stuff. It's just the normal work stress, nothing excessive across the board.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling unusually tired or low on energy lately?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I really have. It's... well, by the time I get home from the office, my energy is just completely gone. Even really simple things, like walking out to check the mail or just loading the dishwasher, they feel like way more effort than they should. I'm just drained by the end of the day.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling worthless lately?\n\nPatient: Well, yeah, actually. That's kind of why I decided to come in. I'm just drained by the end of the day.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling worthless lately?\n\nPatient: Well, yeah, actually. That's kind of why I decided to come in. I just keep beating myself up lately, thinking that I'm not really senior material at work. Most days lately, I just have this... heavy, low feeling. It's like I'm just going through the motions at work and at home, and everything just feels a bit dull, you know? Like the color's been turned down on everything.\n\nDoctor: Have you lost interest or enjoyment in things you usually like to do?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. Usually, I really look forward to grilling on the weekend, or going fishing, or even just putting on a record. But lately... I don't know, I just leave the stuff sitting there. I just don't really care to do any of it right now.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts that life isn’t worth living, or thoughts of hurting yourself?\n\nPatient: Well, I haven't made any plans to hurt myself or anything like that. But... I'm mostly just grabbing a few bites here and there, or completely skipping supper altogether. I've even noticed my belt is a little looser lately, and I haven't been trying to lose weight or anything.\n\nDoctor: Have you been moving or speaking noticeably slower than usual lately?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I haven't noticed anything like that, and nobody at work or anything has mentioned me talking slower or moving different. I just feel tired, but I'm still moving around at my normal pace, I guess.\n\nDoctor: Have these feelings been making it hard for you to function at work or at home?\n\nPatient: I mean, I'm still making it to work, yeah. I haven't missed any days. But I've definitely had to double-check myself a lot more on my projects just to make sure I'm doing things right. And outside of work... I guess I've pulled back from my friends and family quite a bit. I just don't really trust my mood or my judgment lately, so it's been easier to just keep my distance.\n\nDoctor: At any point in the last month, did there come a time when these symptoms stayed present every day for two weeks straight?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure, to be honest. I mean, I haven't been tracking it on a calendar or anything. It definitely feels like it's been going on for a while, but... I don't know if I could say it's been every single day for two solid weeks.\n\nDoctor: Do these feelings seem stronger than you would expect from being asked to cover the senior analyst duties?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure. I mean, taking on those senior duties is a lot of extra pressure, so feeling stressed and run down kind of makes sense to me. Other times, I might actually get enough hours in, but I still wake up feeling completely worn out, like I didn't sleep a wink.\n\nDoctor: Have you been having trouble concentrating lately?\n\nPatient: I don't think so, no.
